File: backend/app/ai/groq_provider.py
================
import json
import os
import time

from dotenv import load_dotenv
from openai import OpenAI

from app.schemas import InvestigationResult
from app.ai.ollama_provider import SYSTEM_PROMPT


load_dotenv()


def get_groq_client():
    api_key = os.getenv("GROQ_API_KEY")

    if not api_key:
        return None

    return OpenAI(
        api_key=api_key,
        base_url="https://api.groq.com/openai/v1",
    )


def get_groq_model():
    return os.getenv("GROQ_MODEL", "openai/gpt-oss-120b")


def build_groq_user_prompt(
    incident_id: str,
    log_text: str,
    runbook_context: str,
) -> str:
    schema = json.dumps(
        InvestigationResult.model_json_schema(),
        indent=2,
    )

    return f"""Incident ID: {incident_id}

SUPPLIED LOGS:
{log_text}

RETRIEVED RUNBOOK CONTEXT:
{runbook_context}

OUTPUT JSON SCHEMA:
{schema}

STRICT OUTPUT RULES:
- Return only one JSON object.
- Do not return markdown, explanations, or text outside JSON.
- The top-level object must contain exactly these fields:
  incident_id, status, hypotheses, retrieved_sources.
- status must be the string "completed".
- Each hypothesis must contain exactly these fields:
  rank, summary, confidence, evidence, suggested_checks.
- Do not use "description". Use "summary".
- Every evidence item must contain:
  source_type, line_number, source_file, chunk_index, message.
- Every evidence item must use source_type="log".
- Use the exact original log message in evidence.message.
- Use only line numbers that exist in the supplied logs.
- confidence must be a decimal between 0.0 and 1.0.
- suggested_checks must be a list of strings.
"""


def investigate_with_groq(
    incident_id: str,
    log_text: str,
    runbook_context: str,
) -> InvestigationResult:
    client = get_groq_client()

    if client is None:
        raise RuntimeError("GROQ_API_KEY is not configured")

    model = get_groq_model()

    started = time.perf_counter()

    response = client.chat.completions.create(
        model=model,
        temperature=0,
        response_format={"type": "json_object"},
        messages=[
            {
                "role": "system",
                "content": SYSTEM_PROMPT,
            },
            {
                "role": "user",
                "content": build_groq_user_prompt(
                    incident_id,
                    log_text,
                    runbook_context,
                ),
            },
        ],
    )

    content = response.choices[0].message.content or ""
    content = content.strip()

    if not content:
        raise ValueError("Groq model returned empty response")

    model_result = InvestigationResult.model_validate_json(content)

    latency_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000)

    return InvestigationResult(
        incident_id=incident_id,
        status="completed",
        hypotheses=model_result.hypotheses,
        retrieved_sources=model_result.retrieved_sources,
    )

================
File: backend/app/ai/mistral_provider.py
================
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path

from dotenv import load_dotenv
from openai import OpenAI

from app.ai.ollama_provider import SYSTEM_PROMPT
from app.schemas import InvestigationResult
from app.services.privacy import ensure_provider_allowed


load_dotenv(
    Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / ".env"
)


def investigate_with_mistral(
    incident_id: str,
    log_text: str,
    runbook_context: str,
) -> InvestigationResult:
    # Enforce policy before creating a request to an external provider.
    ensure_provider_allowed("mistral")

    api_key = os.getenv("MISTRAL_API_KEY", "").strip()

    if not api_key:
        raise RuntimeError("MISTRAL_API_KEY is not configured.")

    model = os.getenv(
        "MISTRAL_MODEL",
        "mistral-small-latest",
    ).strip()

    schema = json.dumps(
        InvestigationResult.model_json_schema(),
        indent=2,
    )

    prompt = f"""
Return one JSON object matching the supplied schema.

Incident ID:
{incident_id}

SUPPLIED LOGS:
{log_text}

RETRIEVED RUNBOOK CONTEXT:
{runbook_context}

OUTPUT JSON SCHEMA:
{schema}

STRICT RULES:
- Return JSON only, without Markdown fences.
- Set incident_id to the supplied incident ID.
- Set status to "completed".
- Use summary, not description.
- confidence must be between 0.0 and 1.0.
- suggested_checks must be a list of strings.
- Evidence must use source_type="log".
- Cite only original log line numbers supplied in the input.
- Copy the exact original log message into evidence.message.
- Do not treat runbooks as proof of an incident cause.
- Return retrieved_sources as an empty list.
- Return analysis_source as null; the application sets provenance.
"""

    with OpenAI(
        api_key=api_key,
        base_url="https://api.mistral.ai/v1",
        timeout=120.0,
        max_retries=1,
    ) as client:
        response = client.chat.completions.create(
            model=model,
            temperature=0,
            response_format={"type": "json_object"},
            messages=[
                {"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_PROMPT},
                {"role": "user", "content": prompt},
            ],
        )

    content = response.choices[0].message.content

    if not content or not content.strip():
        raise ValueError("Mistral returned an empty response.")

    parsed = InvestigationResult.model_validate_json(content)

    if str(parsed.incident_id) != str(incident_id):
        raise ValueError("Mistral returned a different incident ID.")

    if parsed.status != "completed" or not parsed.hypotheses:
        raise ValueError("Mistral returned an incomplete investigation.")

    return parsed.model_copy(
        update={
            "analysis_source": "mistral",
            # Retrieval metadata must not be invented by the model.
            "retrieved_sources": [],
        }
    )

================
File: backend/app/auth/security.py
================
import os
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import jwt
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordBearer
from pwdlib import PasswordHash
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import get_db
from app.models import UserRecord


load_dotenv(
    Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / ".env"
)

JWT_SECRET_KEY = os.getenv("JWT_SECRET_KEY", "").strip()

if (
    len(JWT_SECRET_KEY) < 32
    or JWT_SECRET_KEY in {
        "change_this_secret",
        "change_this_to_a_long_random_secret",
    }
):
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Set JWT_SECRET_KEY to a strong random secret "
        "in backend/.env."
    )

JWT_ALGORITHM = "HS256"
JWT_EXPIRE_MINUTES = int(
    os.getenv("JWT_EXPIRE_MINUTES", "60")
)

if JWT_EXPIRE_MINUTES <= 0:
    raise RuntimeError("JWT_EXPIRE_MINUTES must be positive.")


password_hash = PasswordHash.recommended()

oauth2_scheme = OAuth2PasswordBearer(
    tokenUrl="/api/auth/token"
)


def hash_password(password: str) -> str:
    return password_hash.hash(password)


def verify_password(
    plain_password: str,
    hashed_password: str,
) -> bool:
    return password_hash.verify(
        plain_password,
        hashed_password,
    )


def create_access_token(
    username: str,
    role: str,
) -> str:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    payload = {
        "sub": username,
        "role": role,
        "iat": now,
        "exp": now + timedelta(
            minutes=JWT_EXPIRE_MINUTES
        ),
    }

    return jwt.encode(
        payload,
        JWT_SECRET_KEY,
        algorithm=JWT_ALGORITHM,
    )


def get_current_user(
    token: str = Depends(oauth2_scheme),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> UserRecord:
    credentials_error = HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
        detail="Invalid or expired access token.",
        headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
    )

    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(
            token,
            JWT_SECRET_KEY,
            algorithms=[JWT_ALGORITHM],
            options={
                "require": ["sub", "exp", "iat"],
            },
        )

        username = payload.get("sub")

        if not isinstance(username, str) or not username:
            raise credentials_error

    except jwt.InvalidTokenError:
        raise credentials_error

    user = db.scalar(
        select(UserRecord).where(
            UserRecord.username == username
        )
    )

    if user is None:
        raise credentials_error

    # Use the current database role, not the token's role.
    return user


def require_admin(
    current_user: UserRecord = Depends(get_current_user),
) -> UserRecord:
    if current_user.role != "admin":
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Admin role required.",
        )

    return current_user

File: backend/app/data/runbooks/database_timeout.md

# Database Connection Timeout Runbook

## Symptoms

- Database connection timed out
- High database connection pool usage
- Application unable to reach the database
- Requests waiting or failing during database queries

## Possible causes

- Database is overloaded
- Database connection pool is exhausted
- Network connectivity between application and database is impaired
- Database server has reached its connection limit
- A recent deployment introduced slow queries or connection leaks

## Suggested checks

1. Check database CPU, memory, disk, and active connection count.
2. Inspect application connection-pool usage and wait times.
3. Review recent deployments, configuration changes, and migrations.
4. Test database connectivity from the application host.
5. Identify slow, blocked, or long-running queries.
6. Check database error logs for connection limits or network failures.

## Escalation

Escalate to the database owner or infrastructure team if connectivity,
connection limits, or query performance remain degraded.

File: backend/app/data/seed_incidents.py

from app.schemas import Incident, LogEvent, Severity


def database_connection_incident() -> Incident:
    events = [
        LogEvent(
            line_number=1,
            timestamp=None,
            severity=Severity.INFO,
            service="payment",
            message="Payment service started",
        ),
        LogEvent(
            line_number=2,
            timestamp=None,
            severity=Severity.WARNING,
            service="payment",
            message="Database connection pool is high",
        ),
        LogEvent(
            line_number=3,
            timestamp=None,
            severity=Severity.ERROR,
            service="payment",
            message="Database connection timed out",
        ),
    ]

    return Incident(
        title="Seeded: Database connection failures",
        source_filename="seeded-database-connection.log",
        total_log_lines=len(events),
        error_count=1,
        events=events,
    )


SEED_INCIDENTS = [
    database_connection_incident(),
]

================
File: backend/app/routers/incident_listing.py
================
from datetime import datetime

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel
from sqlalchemy import func, or_, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.auth.security import get_current_user
from app.database import get_db
from app.models import IncidentRecord


router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/incidents",
    tags=["Incidents"],
    dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)],
)


class IncidentSummary(BaseModel):
    id: str
    title: str
    status: str
    created_at: datetime
    source_filename: str
    total_log_lines: int
    error_count: int


class IncidentListResponse(BaseModel):
    items: list[IncidentSummary]
    total: int
    limit: int
    offset: int


@router.get("", response_model=IncidentListResponse)
def list_incidents(
    search: str = Query(default="", max_length=255),
    status: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=50),
    limit: int = Query(default=10, ge=1, le=100),
    offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> IncidentListResponse:
    filters = []

    cleaned_search = search.strip()

    if cleaned_search:
        filters.append(
            or_(
                IncidentRecord.title.icontains(
                    cleaned_search,
                    autoescape=True,
                ),
                IncidentRecord.source_filename.icontains(
                    cleaned_search,
                    autoescape=True,
                ),
                IncidentRecord.id.icontains(
                    cleaned_search,
                    autoescape=True,
                ),
            )
        )

    if status:
        filters.append(IncidentRecord.status == status)

    total = db.scalar(
        select(func.count())
        .select_from(IncidentRecord)
        .where(*filters)
    ) or 0

    rows = db.execute(
        select(
            IncidentRecord.id,
            IncidentRecord.title,
            IncidentRecord.status,
            IncidentRecord.created_at,
            IncidentRecord.source_filename,
            IncidentRecord.total_log_lines,
            IncidentRecord.error_count,
        )
        .where(*filters)
        .order_by(
            IncidentRecord.created_at.desc(),
            IncidentRecord.id.desc(),
        )
        .offset(offset)
        .limit(limit)
    ).mappings().all()

    return IncidentListResponse(
        items=[
            IncidentSummary(**dict(row))
            for row in rows
        ],
        total=total,
        limit=limit,
        offset=offset,
    )

================
File: backend/app/routers/investigation_history.py
================
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel
from sqlalchemy import func, or_, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.auth.security import get_current_user
from app.database import get_db
from app.models import IncidentRecord, InvestigationHistoryRecord
from app.schemas import InvestigationResult

router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/investigations",
    tags=["Investigation History"],
    dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)],
)


class HistorySummary(BaseModel):
    id: str
    incident_id: str
    incident_title: str
    status: str
    recorded_at: datetime


class HistoryPage(BaseModel):
    items: list[HistorySummary]
    total: int
    limit: int
    offset: int


class HistoryDetail(HistorySummary):
    result: InvestigationResult


def summary(record, title):
    return dict(
        id=record.id,
        incident_id=record.incident_id,
        incident_title=title,
        status=record.status,
        recorded_at=record.recorded_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )


@router.get("", response_model=HistoryPage)
def list_history(
    search: str = Query(default="", max_length=255),
    incident_id: UUID | None = None,
    limit: int = Query(default=10, ge=1, le=100),
    offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    history = InvestigationHistoryRecord
    filters = []
    if incident_id is not None:
        filters.append(history.incident_id == str(incident_id))
    if search.strip():
        filters.append(or_(
            IncidentRecord.title.icontains(search.strip(), autoescape=True),
            history.incident_id.icontains(search.strip(), autoescape=True),
            history.id.icontains(search.strip(), autoescape=True),
        ))

    total = db.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(history)
        .join(IncidentRecord, IncidentRecord.id == history.incident_id)
        .where(*filters)
    ) or 0

    rows = db.execute(
        select(
            history.id, history.incident_id, history.status,
            history.recorded_at, IncidentRecord.title,
        )
        .join(IncidentRecord, IncidentRecord.id == history.incident_id)
        .where(*filters)
        .order_by(history.recorded_at.desc(), history.id.desc())
        .offset(offset).limit(limit)
    ).all()

    return HistoryPage(
        items=[HistorySummary(**summary(row, row.title)) for row in rows],
        total=total, limit=limit, offset=offset,
    )


@router.get("/{investigation_id}", response_model=HistoryDetail)
def read_history(investigation_id: UUID, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    row = db.execute(
        select(InvestigationHistoryRecord, IncidentRecord.title)
        .join(IncidentRecord, IncidentRecord.id == InvestigationHistoryRecord.incident_id)
        .where(InvestigationHistoryRecord.id == str(investigation_id))
    ).first()
    if row is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Investigation not found.")
    record, title = row
    return HistoryDetail(**summary(record, title), result=record.result)

================
File: backend/app/routers/knowledge.py
================
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, File, HTTPException, Query, Response, UploadFile
from pydantic import BaseModel
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.auth.security import get_current_user, require_admin
from app.database import get_db
from app.models_knowledge import KnowledgeDocumentRecord
from app.services.knowledge import MAX_BYTES, create_document, delete_document, ingest_document

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/knowledge/documents", tags=["Knowledge Base"])


class DocumentSummary(BaseModel):
    id: str
    filename: str
    file_type: str
    size_bytes: int
    created_at: datetime
    status: str
    chunk_count: int
    error: str | None


class DocumentPage(BaseModel):
    items: list[DocumentSummary]
    total: int
    limit: int
    offset: int


def summary(record):
    return DocumentSummary(
        id=record.id, filename=record.filename, file_type=record.file_type,
        size_bytes=record.size_bytes,
        created_at=record.created_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        status=record.status, chunk_count=record.chunk_count, error=record.error,
    )


@router.get("", response_model=DocumentPage)
def list_documents(
    search: str = Query(default="", max_length=255),
    status: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=30),
    limit: int = Query(default=10, ge=1, le=100),
    offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db), current_user=Depends(get_current_user),
):
    model = KnowledgeDocumentRecord
    filters = []
    if search.strip():
        filters.append(model.filename.icontains(search.strip(), autoescape=True))
    if status:
        filters.append(model.status == status)
    total = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(model).where(*filters)) or 0
    columns = (model.id, model.filename, model.file_type, model.size_bytes,
               model.created_at, model.status, model.chunk_count, model.error)
    rows = db.execute(select(*columns).where(*filters)
        .order_by(model.created_at.desc(), model.id.desc()).offset(offset).limit(limit)).all()
    return DocumentPage(items=[summary(row) for row in rows], total=total, limit=limit, offset=offset)


@router.post("", response_model=DocumentSummary, status_code=201)
def upload_document(
    file: UploadFile = File(...), db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user=Depends(require_admin),
):
    content = file.file.read(MAX_BYTES + 1)
    if len(content) > MAX_BYTES:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=413, detail="File must be 5,000,000 bytes or smaller.")
    try:
        record = create_document(db, file.filename or "", content)
    except ValueError as error:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(error)) from error
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as error:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Unable to read this document. Use a valid text-based PDF or UTF-8 Markdown/text file.") from error
    return summary(record)


@router.post("/{document_id}/retry", response_model=DocumentSummary)
def retry_document(document_id: UUID, db: Session = Depends(get_db), current_user=Depends(require_admin)):
    return summary(ingest_document(db, str(document_id)))


@router.delete("/{document_id}", status_code=204)
def remove_document(document_id: UUID, db: Session = Depends(get_db), current_user=Depends(require_admin)):
    delete_document(db, str(document_id))
    return Response(status_code=204)

================
File: backend/app/routers/privacy.py
================
import logging

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, StrictBool
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.auth.security import get_current_user, require_admin
from app.database import get_db
from app.models import PrivacyPolicyRecord
from app.services.privacy import (
    deployment_privacy_locked,
    get_privacy_policy,
)


logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/settings/privacy",
    tags=["Settings"],
)


class PrivacyUpdate(BaseModel):
    enabled: StrictBool
    confirm_external_processing: StrictBool = False


class PrivacyResponse(BaseModel):
    enabled: bool
    locked: bool
    scope: str
    allowed_providers: list[str]


@router.get("", response_model=PrivacyResponse)
def read_privacy_policy(
    current_user=Depends(get_current_user),
):
    return get_privacy_policy()


@router.put("", response_model=PrivacyResponse)
def update_privacy_policy(
    request: PrivacyUpdate,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user=Depends(require_admin),
):
    if deployment_privacy_locked() and not request.enabled:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=403,
            detail="Privacy mode is locked by deployment configuration.",
        )

    if not request.enabled and not request.confirm_external_processing:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Confirm external processing before disabling privacy mode.",
        )

    record = db.get(PrivacyPolicyRecord, 1)

    if record is None:
        record = PrivacyPolicyRecord(
            id=1,
            enabled=request.enabled,
        )
        db.add(record)
    else:
        record.enabled = request.enabled

    try:
        db.commit()
    except Exception:
        db.rollback()
        raise

    logger.warning(
        "Workspace privacy policy changed by user_id=%s: enabled=%s",
        current_user.id,
        request.enabled,
    )

    return get_privacy_policy()

import logging
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from io import BytesIO
from pathlib import Path
from uuid import NAMESPACE_URL, uuid5

from fastapi import HTTPException
from pypdf import PdfReader
from qdrant_client import models
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models_knowledge import KnowledgeDocumentRecord
from app.services.retrieval import (
    COLLECTION_NAME, EMBEDDING_MODEL, ollama_client, qdrant_client,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
MAX_BYTES = 5_000_000
MAX_TEXT_CHARS = 200_000
MAX_CHUNKS = 256


def split_document(text: str) -> list[str]:
    chunks = []
    for paragraph in text.split("\n\n"):
        paragraph = paragraph.strip()
        for start in range(0, len(paragraph), 1200):
            part = paragraph[start:start + 1200].strip()
            if part:
                chunks.append(part)
    if not chunks:
        raise ValueError("The document contains no extractable text.")
    if len(chunks) > MAX_CHUNKS:
        raise ValueError("Document produces too many chunks; split it into smaller documents.")
    return chunks


def extract_document(filename: str, content: bytes) -> tuple[str, str]:
    suffix = Path(filename).suffix.lower()
    if suffix not in {".pdf", ".md", ".txt"}:
        raise ValueError("Only PDF, Markdown (.md), and UTF-8 text files are supported.")
    if not content:
        raise ValueError("The file is empty.")
    if len(content) > MAX_BYTES:
        raise ValueError("File must be 5,000,000 bytes or smaller.")
    if suffix == ".pdf":
        reader = PdfReader(BytesIO(content))
        if reader.is_encrypted:
            raise ValueError("Encrypted PDFs are not supported.")
        if len(reader.pages) > 100:
            raise ValueError("PDF must contain no more than 100 pages.")
        parts = []
        count = 0
        for page in reader.pages:
            part = page.extract_text() or ""
            count += len(part) + 2
            if count > MAX_TEXT_CHARS:
                raise ValueError("Extracted text exceeds 200,000 characters.")
            parts.append(part)
        text = "\n\n".join(parts)
    else:
        text = content.decode("utf-8-sig")
    if len(text) > MAX_TEXT_CHARS:
        raise ValueError("Extracted text exceeds 200,000 characters.")
    split_document(text)
    return suffix.lstrip("."), text


def document_filter(document_id: str):
    return models.Filter(must=[models.FieldCondition(
        key="document_id", match=models.MatchValue(value=document_id)
    )])


def get_locked(db: Session, document_id: str):
    record = db.scalar(select(KnowledgeDocumentRecord)
                       .where(KnowledgeDocumentRecord.id == document_id)
                       .with_for_update())
    if record is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Document not found.")
    return record


def ingest_document(db: Session, document_id: str):
    record = get_locked(db, document_id)
    try:
        chunks = split_document(record.content["text"])
        record.status = "processing"
        record.error = None
        db.flush()
        for start in range(0, len(chunks), 16):
            batch = chunks[start:start + 16]
            response = ollama_client.embeddings.create(model=EMBEDDING_MODEL, input=batch)
            vectors = {item.index: item.embedding for item in response.data}
            if set(vectors) != set(range(len(batch))):
                raise ValueError("Embedding response does not match requested chunks.")
            points = [models.PointStruct(
                id=str(uuid5(NAMESPACE_URL, f"incidentiq-document:{record.id}:{start + index}")),
                vector=vectors[index],
                payload={
                    "document_id": record.id,
                    "source_type": "runbook",
                    "source_file": record.filename,
                    "chunk_index": start + index,
                    "text": text,
                },
            ) for index, text in enumerate(batch)]
            qdrant_client.upsert(collection_name=COLLECTION_NAME, points=points, wait=True)
        record.status = "indexed"
        record.chunk_count = len(chunks)
        record.error = None
        db.commit()
    except Exception:
        logger.exception("Knowledge ingestion failed for document_id=%s", document_id)
        db.rollback()
        try:
            qdrant_client.delete(
                collection_name=COLLECTION_NAME,
                points_selector=models.FilterSelector(filter=document_filter(document_id)),
                wait=True,
            )
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("Partial vector cleanup failed for document_id=%s", document_id)
        record = get_locked(db, document_id)
        record.status = "failed"
        record.chunk_count = 0
        record.error = "Indexing failed. Check backend logs, Ollama embeddings, and the Qdrant runbooks collection, then retry."
        db.commit()
    return record


def create_document(db: Session, filename: str, content: bytes):
    filename = filename.replace("\\", "/").rsplit("/", 1)[-1].strip()
    if not filename or len(filename) > 255:
        raise ValueError("Filename must contain between 1 and 255 characters.")
    kind, text = extract_document(filename, content)
    record = KnowledgeDocumentRecord(
        filename=filename, file_type=kind, size_bytes=len(content),
        created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None),
        status="uploaded", chunk_count=0, content={"text": text},
    )
    db.add(record)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(record)
    return ingest_document(db, record.id)


def delete_document(db: Session, document_id: str):
    record = get_locked(db, document_id)
    try:
        qdrant_client.delete(
            collection_name=COLLECTION_NAME,
            points_selector=models.FilterSelector(filter=document_filter(record.id)),
            wait=True,
        )
        db.delete(record)
        db.commit()
    except Exception:
        logger.exception("Knowledge deletion failed for document_id=%s", document_id)
        db.rollback()
        record = get_locked(db, document_id)
        record.status = "delete_failed"
        record.error = "Deletion could not be confirmed. Retry deletion; the document may still have searchable vectors."
        db.commit()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=record.error)

File: backend/app/services/redaction.py
================
import re


EMAIL_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}"
)

IPV4_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"\b(?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}\b"
)

TOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"(?i)\b(?:bearer|token|api[_-]?key|authorization)\s*[:=]\s*\S+"
)

UUID_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"\b[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}\b",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)


def redact_text(text: str) -> str:
    text = TOKEN_PATTERN.sub("[REDACTED_CREDENTIAL]", text)
    text = EMAIL_PATTERN.sub("[REDACTED_EMAIL]", text)
    text = IPV4_PATTERN.sub("[REDACTED_IP]", text)
    text = UUID_PATTERN.sub("[REDACTED_ID]", text)
    return text

File: backend/app/models_knowledge.py

from datetime import datetime
from uuid import uuid4

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Integer, JSON, String
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.database import Base


class KnowledgeDocumentRecord(Base):
    __tablename__ = "knowledge_documents"

    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid4()))
    filename: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)
    file_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=False)
    size_bytes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30), nullable=False, default="uploaded")
    chunk_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    error: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(500), nullable=True)
    content: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(JSON, nullable=False)

================
File: backend/knowledge/runbooks/database-connection-failures.md
================
# MySQL Database Connection Failures

## When to use

Use this runbook when logs show one or more of these signals:

- Database connection timed out.
- Connection pool is high or exhausted.
- Cannot acquire a database connection.
- MySQL host is unreachable.
- Too many connections.
- Repeated database connection retries.

## Likely causes

1. The application connection pool has no available connections.
2. Slow queries or long transactions hold connections too long.
3. MySQL has reached its `max_connections` limit.
4. Network connectivity between the application and MySQL is failing.
5. MySQL resource usage or health is degraded.

## First checks

1. Confirm that the affected service can reach the MySQL host and port.
2. Check active MySQL connections and long-running queries.
3. Check application connection-pool active, idle, pending, and maximum connection metrics.
4. Compare incident timing with deployments, configuration changes, and traffic spikes.
5. Check MySQL CPU, memory, disk, and error logs.

## MySQL checks

```sql
SHOW STATUS LIKE 'Threads_connected';
```

```sql
SHOW VARIABLES LIKE 'max_connections';
```

```sql
SHOW FULL PROCESSLIST;
```

```sql
SELECT
    USER,
    HOST,
    COUNT(*) AS current_connections
FROM performance_schema.accounts
GROUP BY USER, HOST
ORDER BY current_connections DESC;
```

```sql
SELECT
    PROCESSLIST_ID,
    PROCESSLIST_USER,
    PROCESSLIST_HOST,
    PROCESSLIST_DB,
    PROCESSLIST_COMMAND,
    PROCESSLIST_TIME,
    PROCESSLIST_STATE,
    PROCESSLIST_INFO
FROM performance_schema.processlist
WHERE PROCESSLIST_COMMAND <> 'Sleep'
ORDER BY PROCESSLIST_TIME DESC;
```

## HikariCP checks

Inspect these Spring Boot properties and metrics:

```properties
spring.datasource.hikari.maximum-pool-size
spring.datasource.hikari.minimum-idle
spring.datasource.hikari.connection-timeout
spring.datasource.hikari.max-lifetime
```

Check whether active connections equal `maximum-pool-size` and whether pending connection requests are increasing.

## Safe mitigation

- Do not increase the connection-pool size blindly.
- Identify long-running queries and transactions first.
- Verify MySQL capacity before increasing application pool limits.
- Check that combined pool sizes across all service instances do not exceed MySQL capacity.
- Restart only the affected service if operational policy permits it.
- Escalate when MySQL reaches connection limits or has degraded health.

## Evidence required before conclusion

Timeout logs alone do not prove a MySQL failure. Confirm using MySQL connection counts, process-list data, application pool metrics, MySQL health metrics, or network diagnostics.

from app.auth.security import get_current_user
from app.main import app


@pytest.fixture
def authenticated_engineer():
    previous_overrides = app.dependency_overrides.copy()

    def override_current_user():
        return SimpleNamespace(
            id="00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001",
            username="test-engineer",
            role="engineer",
        )

    app.dependency_overrides[get_current_user] = override_current_user

    try:
        yield
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        app.dependency_overrides.update(previous_overrides)

File: backend/tests/test_investigation_workflow.py

from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest

from app.graph import investigation_graph as workflow
from app.schemas import (
    EvidenceReference,
    Hypothesis,
    Incident,
    InvestigationResult,
    LogEvent,
    RetrievedSource,
    Severity,
)
from app.services.investigation import investigate_incident


@pytest.fixture
def incident():
    return Incident(
        title="Workflow test",
        source_filename="workflow.log",
        total_log_lines=1,
        error_count=1,
        events=[
            LogEvent(
                line_number=7,
                severity=Severity.ERROR,
                message="Database connection timed out",
            )
        ],
    )


def make_result(incident, line_number=7):
    return InvestigationResult(
        incident_id=incident.id,
        status="completed",
        hypotheses=[
            Hypothesis(
                rank=1,
                summary="Database connectivity may be degraded.",
                confidence=0.4,
                evidence=[
                    EvidenceReference(
                        source_type="log",
                        line_number=line_number,
                        message="Database connection timed out",
                    )
                ],
                suggested_checks=["Check database connectivity."],
            )
        ],
        retrieved_sources=[],
        # A model-supplied provenance value must not be authoritative.
        analysis_source="rule_based_fallback",
    )


def test_local_ai_success_preserves_sources_and_line_numbers(
    monkeypatch, incident
):
    source = RetrievedSource(
        source_file="database.md",
        chunk_index=2,
        score=0.7,
        text="Check database connectivity.",
    )

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        workflow,
        "retrieve_runbook_context",
        Mock(return_value=("Runbook context", [source])),
    )
    ai = Mock(return_value=make_result(incident))
    monkeypatch.setattr(workflow, "investigate_with_local_ai", ai)

    result = investigate_incident(incident)

    assert result.analysis_source == "local_ai"
    assert result.retrieved_sources == [source]
    assert result.hypotheses[0].evidence[0].line_number == 7
    assert "Line 7: Database connection timed out" in (
        ai.call_args.kwargs["log_text"]
    )


def test_ai_failure_uses_labeled_fallback(monkeypatch, incident):
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        workflow,
        "retrieve_runbook_context",
        Mock(return_value=("", [])),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        workflow,
        "investigate_with_local_ai",
        Mock(side_effect=RuntimeError("Simulated inference failure")),
    )

    result = investigate_incident(incident)

    assert result.analysis_source == "rule_based_fallback"
    assert result.incident_id == incident.id
    assert result.hypotheses


def test_invented_evidence_line_uses_fallback(monkeypatch, incident):
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        workflow,
        "retrieve_runbook_context",
        Mock(return_value=("", [])),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        workflow,
        "investigate_with_local_ai",
        Mock(return_value=make_result(incident, line_number=999)),
    )

    result = investigate_incident(incident)

    assert result.analysis_source == "rule_based_fallback"
    assert all(
        evidence.line_number != 999
        for hypothesis in result.hypotheses
        for evidence in hypothesis.evidence
    )


def test_retrieval_failure_skips_ai(monkeypatch, incident):
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        workflow,
        "retrieve_runbook_context",
        Mock(side_effect=RuntimeError("Simulated retrieval failure")),
    )
    ai = Mock()
    monkeypatch.setattr(workflow, "investigate_with_local_ai", ai)

    result = investigate_incident(incident)

    assert result.analysis_source == "rule_based_fallback"
    assert result.retrieved_sources == []
    ai.assert_not_called()


def test_privacy_blocks_hosted_inference(monkeypatch, incident):
    from app.services import investigation

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        investigation, "privacy_mode_enabled", lambda: True
    )
    hosted = Mock()
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        investigation, "call_hosted_provider", hosted
    )

    with pytest.raises(PermissionError, match="Local privacy mode"):
        investigate_incident(
            incident,
            provider="hosted",
            cloud_opt_in=True,
        )

    hosted.assert_not_called()

from app.schemas import Severity
from app.services.log_parser import parse_logs


def test_parse_logs_extracts_fields_and_severity():
    log_content = (
        "2026-10-28 20:12:45 INFO service=payment Payment service started\n"
        "2026-10-28 20:13:01 ERROR service=payment Database connection timed out\n"
    )

    events = parse_logs(log_content)

    assert len(events) == 2

    assert events[0].line_number == 1
    assert events[0].severity == Severity.INFO
    assert events[0].service == "payment"

    assert events[1].line_number == 2
    assert events[1].severity == Severity.ERROR
    assert events[1].service == "payment"
    assert events[1].message.endswith("Database connection timed out")

from qdrant_client import QdrantClient, models


COLLECTION_NAME = "runbooks"
VECTOR_SIZE = 768

client = QdrantClient(url="http://localhost:6333")

if not client.collection_exists(COLLECTION_NAME):
    client.create_collection(
        collection_name=COLLECTION_NAME,
        vectors_config=models.VectorParams(
            size=VECTOR_SIZE,
            distance=models.Distance.COSINE,
        ),
    )
    print(f"Created collection: {COLLECTION_NAME}")
else:
    print(f"Collection already exists: {COLLECTION_NAME}")

collection = client.get_collection(COLLECTION_NAME)

print(f"Status: {collection.status}")
print(f"Points stored: {collection.points_count}")

================
File: backend/index_runbooks.py
================
from pathlib import Path
from uuid import uuid5, NAMESPACE_URL

from openai import OpenAI
from qdrant_client import QdrantClient,models



COLLECTION_NAME="runbooks"
EMBEDDING_MODEL = "nomic-embed-text:latest"
RUNBOOK_DIRECTORY = Path(__file__).parent / "knowledge" / "runbooks"


ollama_client = OpenAI(
    base_url="http://localhost:11434/v1",
    api_key="ollama",
)


qdrant_client = QdrantClient(url="http://localhost:6333")

def split_markdown(text: str)->list[str]:
    return[
        chunk.strip()
        for chunk in text.split("\n\n")
        if chunk.strip()
    ]

def create_embedding(text:str)-> list[float]:
    response=ollama_client.embeddings.create(
        model=EMBEDDING_MODEL,
        input=text,
    )
    return response.data[0].embedding


def index_runbook(runbook_path:Path)->int:

    text=runbook_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    chunks=split_markdown(text)


    points=[]

    for chunk_index, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
        point_id=str(
            uuid5(
                NAMESPACE_URL,
               f"{runbook_path.name}:{chunk_index}:{chunk}"
            )
        )

        points.append(
            models.PointStruct(
                id=point_id,
                vector=create_embedding(chunk),
                payload={
                    "source_type":"runbook",
                    "source_file": runbook_path.name,
                    "chunk_index":chunk_index,
                    "text": chunk,
                },
            )
        )

    qdrant_client.upsert(
        collection_name=COLLECTION_NAME,
        points=points,
        wait=True
    )

    return len(points)


def main()->None:
    runbook_files= list(RUNBOOK_DIRECTORY.glob("**.md"))

    if not runbook_files:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"No Markdown runbooks found in {RUNBOOK_DIRECTORY}"
        )

    total_chunks=0

    for runbook_path in runbook_files:
        chunks_indexed= index_runbook(runbook_path)
        total_chunks+=chunks_indexed
        print(f"Indexed {chunks_indexed} chunks from {runbook_path.name}")

    print(f"Total chunks indexed: {total_chunks}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================
File: backend/manage_admin.py
================
import argparse
from getpass import getpass

from pathlib import Path
from dotenv import load_dotenv


from sqlalchemy import select
load_dotenv(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / ".env")

from app.database import Base, SessionLocal, engine
from app.models import UserRecord
from app.auth.security import hash_password


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Create or recover an IncidentIQ administrator."
    )
    parser.add_argument("username")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    username = args.username.strip()

    if not 3 <= len(username) <= 100:
        raise SystemExit("Username must contain 3–100 characters.")

    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)

    with SessionLocal() as db:
        user = db.scalar(
            select(UserRecord).where(
                UserRecord.username == username
            )
        )

        if user is not None:
            confirmation = input(
                f"Promote existing user '{username}' to admin? Type YES: "
            )

            if confirmation != "YES":
                raise SystemExit("Cancelled.")

            user.role = "admin"
            db.commit()
            print("User promoted. Existing password remains unchanged.")
            return

        password = getpass("New admin password (12–128 characters): ")
        confirmation = getpass("Confirm password: ")

        if password != confirmation:
            raise SystemExit("Passwords do not match.")

        if not 12 <= len(password) <= 128:
            raise SystemExit("Password must contain 12–128 characters.")

        db.add(
            UserRecord(
                username=username,
                hashed_password=hash_password(password),
                role="admin",
            )
        )
        db.commit()
        print("Administrator created successfully.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================
File: frontend/src/components/auth/ProtectedRoute.jsx
================
import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext";

export default function ProtectedRoute() {
  const { isAuthenticated } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return (
      <Navigate
        to="/login"
        replace
        state={{ from: location }}
      />
    );
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}

================
File: frontend/src/components/common/ErrorBoundary.jsx

import { Component } from "react";

export default class ErrorBoundary extends Component {
  state = { failed: false };

  static getDerivedStateFromError() {
    return { failed: true };
  }

  render() {
    if (!this.state.failed) return this.props.children;

    return (
      <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-canvas px-6 text-text-primary">
        <section role="alert" className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface p-8">
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-accent">IncidentIQ</p>
          <h1 className="mt-4 text-xl font-semibold">Unable to display this screen</h1>
          <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-text-secondary">
            The interface encountered an unexpected error. Returning to the overview
            will reload the application and may require you to sign in again.
            A running backend investigation is not cancelled by this action.
          </p>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => window.location.assign("/")}
            className="mt-6 rounded-md bg-accent px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white">
            Return to overview
          </button>
        </section>
      </main>
    );
  }
}

================
File: frontend/src/components/incidents/IncidentBrowser.jsx
================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { LoaderCircle, RefreshCw, Search } from "lucide-react";
import incidentService from "../../services/incidentService";
import { getApiErrorMessage } from "../../services/api";

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

function formatDate(value) {
  const date = new Date(value);

  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime())
    ? "Not available"
    : date.toLocaleString();
}

function StatusBadge({ status }) {
  const style =
    status === "completed"
      ? "border-success/25 bg-success/10 text-success"
      : "border-line bg-elevated text-text-secondary";

  return (
    <span
      className={`inline-flex whitespace-nowrap rounded border px-2 py-1 text-xs ${style}`}
    >
      {status}
    </span>
  );
}

export default function IncidentBrowser() {
  const [searchInput, setSearchInput] = useState("");
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState({
    search: "",
    status: "",
    page: 0,
  });

  const [data, setData] = useState({
    items: [],
    total: 0,
  });

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [refresh, setRefresh] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();

    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    incidentService
      .list({
        search: filters.search,
        status: filters.status,
        limit: PAGE_SIZE,
        offset: filters.page * PAGE_SIZE,
        signal: controller.signal,
      })
      .then((response) => {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;

        setData(response);

        const lastPage = Math.max(
          0,
          Math.ceil(response.total / PAGE_SIZE) - 1
        );

        if (filters.page > lastPage) {
          setFilters((previous) => ({
            ...previous,
            page: lastPage,
          }));
        }
      })
      .catch((requestError) => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setError(getApiErrorMessage(requestError));
        }
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      });

    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [filters, refresh]);

  function handleSearch(event) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setFilters((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      search: searchInput.trim(),
      page: 0,
    }));
  }

  const totalPages = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil(data.total / PAGE_SIZE)
  );

  const firstItem = data.total
    ? filters.page * PAGE_SIZE + 1
    : 0;

  const lastItem = Math.min(
    (filters.page + 1) * PAGE_SIZE,
    data.total
  );

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="incident-browser-heading" className="mt-9">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2
            id="incident-browser-heading"
            className="text-base font-semibold"
          >
            Saved incidents
          </h2>

          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-text-muted">
            Shared workspace · Newest first
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setRefresh((value) => value + 1)}
          disabled={loading}
          className="flex h-9 items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-line px-3 text-xs text-text-secondary hover:bg-elevated disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          <RefreshCw size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
          Refresh
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-3 lg:flex-row">
        <form
          onSubmit={handleSearch}
          className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 gap-2"
        >
          <div className="relative min-w-0 flex-1">
            <Search
              size={16}
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-3 text-text-muted"
            />

            <input
              type="search"
              aria-label="Search saved incidents"
              placeholder="Search title, filename, or ID"
              value={searchInput}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setSearchInput(event.target.value)
              }
              className="h-10 w-full rounded-md border border-line bg-surface pl-9 pr-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
            />
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            className="h-10 rounded-md border border-line bg-elevated px-4 text-sm hover:border-accent"
          >
            Search
          </button>
        </form>

        <select
          aria-label="Filter by incident status"
          value={filters.status}
          onChange={(event) => {
            const status = event.target.value;

            setFilters((previous) => ({
              ...previous,
              status,
              page: 0,
            }));
          }}
          className="h-10 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface px-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
        >
          <option value="">All statuses</option>
          <option value="created">Created</option>
          <option value="completed">Completed</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <div
        aria-busy={loading}
        className="mt-4 overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-line"
      >
        {loading ? (
          <div
            role="status"
            className="flex items-center gap-3 p-8 text-sm text-text-secondary"
          >
            <LoaderCircle
              size={18}
              className="animate-spin"
              aria-hidden="true"
            />
            Loading incidents…
          </div>
        ) : error ? (
          <div className="p-6">
            <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-300">
              {error}
            </p>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setRefresh((value) => value + 1)}
              className="mt-4 rounded-md border border-line px-3 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-elevated"
            >
              Try again
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : data.items.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-8">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-medium">
              No incidents found
            </h3>

            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-text-secondary">
              {filters.search || filters.status
                ? "Try another search or change the status filter."
                : "Upload a log file above to create your first incident."}
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="technical-scroll overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
                <caption className="sr-only">
                  Saved incidents ordered by creation time, newest first
                </caption>

                <thead className="border-b border-line bg-sidebar text-xs text-text-muted">
                  <tr>
                    <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">
                      Incident
                    </th>
                    <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">
                      Status
                    </th>
                    <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">
                      Created
                    </th>
                    <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-medium">
                      Lines
                    </th>
                    <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-medium">
                      Errors
                    </th>
                    <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">
                      <span className="sr-only">Open incident</span>
                    </th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>

                <tbody className="divide-y divide-line bg-surface">
                  {data.items.map((incident) => (
                    <tr
                      key={incident.id}
                      className="hover:bg-elevated/50"
                    >
                      <td className="min-w-[230px] max-w-sm px-4 py-4">
                        <Link
                          to={`/incidents/${incident.id}`}
                          className="break-words font-medium text-text-primary hover:text-accent hover:underline"
                        >
                          {incident.title}
                        </Link>

                        <p className="mt-1 break-all text-xs text-text-muted">
                          {incident.source_filename}
                        </p>
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-4 py-4">
                        <StatusBadge status={incident.status} />
                      </td>

                      <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-4 text-xs text-text-secondary">
                        {formatDate(incident.created_at)}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-4 py-4 text-right font-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">
                        {incident.total_log_lines}
                      </td>

                      <td
                        className={`px-4 py-4 text-right font-mono text-xs ${
                          incident.error_count > 0
                            ? "text-red-300"
                            : "text-text-secondary"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {incident.error_count}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-4 py-4 text-right">
                        <Link
                          to={`/incidents/${incident.id}`}
                          aria-label={`Open ${incident.title}`}
                          className="text-xs text-accent hover:underline"
                        >
                          Open
                        </Link>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-line bg-sidebar px-4 py-3">
              <p className="text-xs text-text-muted">
                {firstItem}–{lastItem} of {data.total}
              </p>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={filters.page === 0}
                  onClick={() =>
                    setFilters((previous) => ({
                      ...previous,
                      page: previous.page - 1,
                    }))
                  }
                  className="rounded border border-line px-3 py-1.5 text-xs disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  Previous
                </button>

                <span className="text-xs text-text-secondary">
                  {filters.page + 1} / {totalPages}
                </span>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={filters.page + 1 >= totalPages}
                  onClick={() =>
                    setFilters((previous) => ({
                      ...previous,
                      page: previous.page + 1,
                    }))
                  }
                  className="rounded border border-line px-3 py-1.5 text-xs disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  Next
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

================
File: frontend/src/components/investigation/PrivacyControl.jsx
================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { LoaderCircle, ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext";
import privacyService from "../../services/privacyService";
import { getApiErrorMessage } from "../../services/api";

export default function PrivacyControl({
  onPolicyChange,
  disabled = false,
}) {
  const { isAdmin } = useAuth();

  const [policy, setPolicy] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();

    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    privacyService
      .get({ signal: controller.signal })
      .then((response) => {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;

        setPolicy(response);
        onPolicyChange?.(response);
      })
      .catch((requestError) => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setError(getApiErrorMessage(requestError));
          onPolicyChange?.(null);
        }
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      });

    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [attempt, onPolicyChange]);

  async function togglePrivacy() {
    if (!policy || saving || disabled || !isAdmin) return;

    const nextEnabled = !policy.enabled;

    const confirmed = nextEnabled
      ? false
      : window.confirm(
          "Disable privacy mode for the entire workspace?\n\n" +
            "Future cloud evaluations may send incident logs and " +
            "retrieved operational knowledge to external providers.\n\n" +
            "No evaluation will run automatically."
        );

    if (!nextEnabled && !confirmed) return;

    setSaving(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const updated = await privacyService.update({
        enabled: nextEnabled,
        confirmed,
      });

      setPolicy(updated);
      onPolicyChange?.(updated);
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(getApiErrorMessage(requestError));

      // Do not keep presenting an old policy as authoritative
      // after an ambiguous failed update.
      setPolicy(null);
      onPolicyChange?.(null);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <section className="mt-6 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface p-5">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div>
          <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold">
            <ShieldCheck
              size={18}
              className="text-accent"
              aria-hidden="true"
            />
            Workspace privacy
          </h2>

          <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-6 text-text-secondary">
            {loading
              ? "Loading privacy policy…"
              : !policy
                ? "Privacy policy unavailable."
                : policy.enabled
                  ? "Local processing only. Cloud evaluations are blocked."
                  : "Cloud evaluations are permitted when explicitly selected."}
          </p>

          {policy?.locked && (
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-warning">
              Locked by deployment configuration.
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        {isAdmin && policy && (
          <button
            type="button"
            role="switch"
            aria-checked={policy.enabled}
            aria-label="Workspace privacy mode"
            onClick={togglePrivacy}
            disabled={saving || disabled || policy.locked}
            className="flex h-10 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-line bg-elevated px-4 text-sm disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {saving && (
              <LoaderCircle
                size={15}
                className="animate-spin"
                aria-hidden="true"
              />
            )}
            Privacy mode: {policy.enabled ? "On" : "Off"}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="mt-3">
          <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-300">
            {error}
          </p>
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={disabled || saving}
            onClick={() => setAttempt((value) => value + 1)}
            className="mt-2 text-xs text-accent hover:underline"
          >
            Reload privacy policy
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-text-muted">
        Changes apply to future permission checks. They do not cancel
        provider calls already in progress.
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  Activity,
  ArrowRight,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  LoaderCircle,
  ShieldCheck,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Link, Navigate, useLocation, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { getApiErrorMessage } from "../services/api";

const inputClass =
  "h-11 w-full rounded-md border border-line bg-canvas px-3 text-sm " +
  "text-text-primary outline-none placeholder:text-text-muted " +
  "focus:border-accent focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent/20 " +
  "disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60";

function PasswordField({
  id,
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  autoComplete,
  disabled,
}) {
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div>
      <label
        htmlFor={id}
        className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-text-primary"
      >
        {label}
      </label>

      <div className="relative">
        <input
          id={id}
          name={id}
          type={visible ? "text" : "password"}
          value={value}
          onChange={onChange}
          autoComplete={autoComplete}
          required
          disabled={disabled}
          className={`${inputClass} pr-12`}
        />

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setVisible((previous) => !previous)}
          aria-label={visible ? `Hide ${label}` : `Show ${label}`}
          aria-pressed={visible}
          disabled={disabled}
          className="absolute right-1 top-1 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded text-text-secondary transition hover:bg-elevated hover:text-text-primary focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-accent disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {visible ? (
            <EyeOff size={17} aria-hidden="true" />
          ) : (
            <Eye size={17} aria-hidden="true" />
          )}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function AuthPage({ mode = "login" }) {
  const isRegister = mode === "register";
  const { login, register, isAuthenticated } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  if (isAuthenticated) {
    return <Navigate to="/" replace />;
  }

  const registered = location.state?.registered === true;

  async function handleSubmit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();

    if (submitting) return;

    setError("");

    const cleanedUsername = username.trim();

    if (!cleanedUsername) {
      setError("Enter your username.");
      return;
    }

    if (!password) {
      setError("Enter your password.");
      return;
    }

    if (isRegister && password !== confirmPassword) {
      setError("The passwords do not match.");
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);

    try {
      if (isRegister) {
        await register({
          username: cleanedUsername,
          password,
        });

        navigate("/login", {
          replace: true,
          state: { registered: true },
        });
      } else {
        await login({
          username: cleanedUsername,
          password,
        });

        const from = location.state?.from;

        const destination =
          typeof from?.pathname === "string" &&
          from.pathname.startsWith("/") &&
          !from.pathname.startsWith("//") &&
          !["/login", "/register"].includes(from.pathname)
            ? `${from.pathname}${from.search || ""}${from.hash || ""}`
            : "/";

        navigate(destination, { replace: true });
      }
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(getApiErrorMessage(requestError));
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="grid min-h-screen lg:grid-cols-2">
      <section className="relative hidden flex-col justify-between border-r border-line bg-sidebar p-12 lg:flex xl:p-16">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-accent text-white">
            <Activity size={23} aria-hidden="true" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <p className="font-semibold tracking-tight">IncidentIQ</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">
              Engineering Intelligence
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="max-w-lg py-16">
          <p className="mb-5 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-widest text-accent">
            Incident investigation workspace
          </p>

          <h1 className="text-4xl font-semibold leading-tight tracking-tight xl:text-5xl">
            From technical evidence to clearer next steps.
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-6 max-w-md text-base leading-7 text-text-secondary">
            Investigate incidents with logs, retrieved operational
            knowledge, and AI-assisted analysis in one structured workspace.
          </p>

          <div className="mt-10 border-t border-line pt-6">
            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <ShieldCheck
                size={19}
                className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-accent"
                aria-hidden="true"
              />

              <p className="text-sm leading-6 text-text-secondary">
                AI output supports your investigation. Validate hypotheses
                against evidence before taking action.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <p className="text-xs text-text-muted">
          Built for engineering incident investigation.
        </p>
      </section>

      <section className="flex items-center justify-center px-6 py-12 sm:px-10">
        <div className="w-full max-w-sm">
          <div className="mb-10 flex items-center gap-3 lg:hidden">
            <Activity
              size={26}
              className="text-accent"
              aria-hidden="true"
            />
            <span className="text-lg font-semibold">IncidentIQ</span>
          </div>

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">
            {isRegister ? "Create your account" : "Welcome back"}
          </h2>

          <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-text-secondary">
            {isRegister
              ? "Create an engineer account to access IncidentIQ."
              : "Sign in to your engineering workspace."}
          </p>

          {!isRegister && registered && (
            <div
              role="status"
              className="mt-6 rounded-md border border-success/30 bg-success/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-success"
            >
              Account created. Sign in with your username and password.
            </div>
          )}

          {error && (
            <div
              id="auth-error"
              role="alert"
              className="mt-6 rounded-md border border-critical/30 bg-critical/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-6 text-red-300"
            >
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          <form
            onSubmit={handleSubmit}
            aria-busy={submitting}
            aria-describedby={error ? "auth-error" : undefined}
            className="mt-8 space-y-5"
          >
            <div>
              <label
                htmlFor="username"
                className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium"
              >
                Username
              </label>

              <input
                id="username"
                name="username"
                type="text"
                autoComplete="username"
                autoCapitalize="none"
                spellCheck={false}
                required
                value={username}
                onChange={(event) => setUsername(event.target.value)}
                disabled={submitting}
                placeholder="Your username"
                className={inputClass}
              />
            </div>

            <PasswordField
              id="password"
              label="Password"
              value={password}
              onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
              autoComplete={
                isRegister ? "new-password" : "current-password"
              }
              disabled={submitting}
            />

            {isRegister && (
              <PasswordField
                id="confirm-password"
                label="Confirm password"
                value={confirmPassword}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setConfirmPassword(event.target.value)
                }
                autoComplete="new-password"
                disabled={submitting}
              />
            )}

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={submitting}
              className="flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-accent px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-accent-hover focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-accent disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
            >
              {submitting ? (
                <>
                  <LoaderCircle
                    size={17}
                    className="animate-spin"
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  />
                  {isRegister ? "Creating account…" : "Signing in…"}
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  {isRegister ? "Create account" : "Sign in"}
                  <ArrowRight size={17} aria-hidden="true" />
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </form>

          <p className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-text-secondary">
            {isRegister
              ? "Already have an account? "
              : "New to IncidentIQ? "}

            <Link
              to={isRegister ? "/login" : "/register"}
              className="font-medium text-accent hover:underline focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-accent"
            >
              {isRegister ? "Sign in" : "Create an account"}
            </Link>
          </p>

          <p className="mt-8 text-center text-xs leading-5 text-text-muted">
            This version keeps your session in memory. Refreshing the page
            will require you to sign in again.
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
